## Weekly market prices of 30 goods against pre-war, and the Gaza price index (Gaza Chamber of Commerce)

أسعار 30 سلعة أسبوعياً مقارنة بما قبل الحرب، ومؤشر أسعار غزة (غرفة تجارة غزة)

- **Key:** `gazacham_prices` · **publisher:** Gaza Chamber of Commerce and Industry — Gaza Movement of Goods weekly reports (`gaza_chamber`)
- **Rows:** 3,809 held · 3,809 a question can reach · 0 exportable · 0 older values kept
- **Span:** 2024-08-29 → 2026-09-13 · **series:** 103 · **cadence:** irregular
- **Freshness:** fresh: read on its own rhythm and within its allowance (last information 2026-09-13)
- **Terms:** no-license-found · partners: withheld (no-redistribution); the figure is cited with its source · terms: https://gazacham.ps/InformationCenter/Reports
- **Credit:** Trucks into Gaza: Gaza Chamber of Commerce and Industry, Gaza Movement of Goods weekly reports (gazacham.ps). All rights reserved.
- **Scope:** Week prices of 30 goods in Gaza's markets from 1 April 2025, each with the pre-war price the report compares it with; the weeks a table printed NA for a good; the GCPI daily from 27 May 2025, by the index's weeks from 31 January 2025 and by month from February 2025. Weeks 62-65 (October 2025) were priced for North and South apart and are not here, nor are weeks 1-35.
- **Read before use:** A week's average of the prices the Gaza Chamber of Commerce recorded in Gaza's markets, dated by the week's first day (its last day is in the row), in the unit the Chamber prices the good in; each row also carries the pre-war price the report compares it with (prewar_price). The reports price the Strip as one market from week 27 (28 January 2025); before that, and in weeks 62 to 65 (October 2025), they priced the north and the south apart, which are series of their own. Rows before week 36 state no unit: theirs is the later tables' for the same pre-war price (the row's basis says so). Not WFP's monthly retail price: never one series.
- **Read before use:** A week's average of the prices the Gaza Chamber of Commerce recorded in the northern part of the Gaza Strip, dated by the week's first day (its last day is in the row). The reports priced the north and the south apart from August 2024 to January 2025, and again in October 2025, when the north was cut off from the south. The rows state no unit: the unit is the later tables' for the same pre-war price, or, where a report's pre-war price differs and the unit is not in doubt (per kilo or litre), the report's own (the row's basis says which). Where the same report's daily table gives another average, it sits beside the figure (daily_table_average). Not WFP's monthly retail price: never one series.
- **Read before use:** The Chamber's price table printed NA for this good in these weeks, in place of a price. The reports do not define NA; their summaries say, in the same weeks, that such goods were no longer found in the markets. A week with no row here is not a week the good was found: it is a week with a price, or with no table.
- **Read before use:** A week's average of the prices the Gaza Chamber of Commerce recorded in the south of the Gaza Strip, dated by the week's first day (its last day is in the row). The reports priced the north and the south apart from August 2024 to January 2025, and again in October 2025, when the north was cut off from the south. The rows state no unit: the unit is the later tables' for the same pre-war price, or, where a report's pre-war price differs and the unit is not in doubt (per kilo or litre), the report's own (the row's basis says which). Where the same report's daily table gives another average, it sits beside the figure (daily_table_average). Not WFP's monthly retail price: never one series.
- **Read before use:** The Chamber's basket is rice 1.38 kg, vegetable oil 1.37 l, sugar 1 kg, flour 12.65 kg, tomato 3.5 kg, cucumber 3.45 kg, onion 3.5 kg, potato 2.5 kg, soap, sanitary towels, washing liquid and dishwashing liquid. Daily from 27 May 2025; 29 September to 26 October 2025 were measured for North and South apart and are not here.
- **Read before use:** The Chamber's basket is rice 1.38 kg, vegetable oil 1.37 l, sugar 1 kg, flour 12.65 kg, tomato 3.5 kg, cucumber 3.45 kg, onion 3.5 kg, potato 2.5 kg, soap, sanitary towels, washing liquid and dishwashing liquid. The Chamber's monthly value, February 2025 on. A month the newest report gives only 'up to' a day is left until a report gives it whole; where two reports print a month differently, the later report's value is held.
- **Ask:** MCP `databank(category="food", indicator="price_gazacham.basket_cost_daily")` · REST `/v2/databank/food?indicator=price_gazacham.basket_cost_daily`

## Gaza market prices by day, as a market channel posts them (68 goods by area)

أسعار سوق غزة يومياً كما تنشرها قناة الأسعار (68 سلعة حسب المنطقة)

- **Key:** `market_channel_prices` · **publisher:** أسعار السوق - غزة (Telegram channel @asarsook) (`tg_asarsook`)
- **Rows:** 1,848 held · 1,848 a question can reach · 0 exportable · 0 older values kept
- **Span:** 2024-11-19 → 2026-09-01 · **series:** 280 · **cadence:** irregular
- **Freshness:** closed collection: complete, nothing more will come (last information 2026-09-01)
- **Terms:** no-license-found · partners: withheld (no-redistribution); the figure is cited with its source · terms: https://t.me/s/asarsook
- **Credit:** Gaza market prices: the Telegram channel «أسعار السوق - غزة» (@asarsook).
- **Scope:** One list's price per good, area and day (not an average or a survey): the north, Gaza City, the middle area, the south, or the Strip where a list names no area; the price paid by app as series of their own; the days a list marked a good missing. Not read: exchange rates, adverts, commentary, goods priced by the uqiya or the ratl or in packs of no stated size.
- **Read before use:** The price of this good as the Telegram channel «أسعار السوق - غزة» (@asarsook) posted it for the day: one list's price, not an average or a survey, and the channel states no method. The list named no area: it may be any of the Strip's markets. A list is dated by the day it prints, else by the post's day in Gaza. Where a list prints no unit the price is per kilo, as these lists sell produce, meat and fish (the row's basis says which); a printed range is held as its midpoint, both ends beside it (range_printed); a different price the same day sits beside the figure (other_prices_same_day). Not the Chamber's weekly average or WFP's monthly price: never one series.

## People in each IPC phase of acute food insecurity in Gaza, by analysis and area (IPC)

السكان في كل مرحلة من مراحل انعدام الأمن الغذائي الحاد في غزة، حسب التحليل والمنطقة (IPC)

- **Key:** `ipc_phases` · **publisher:** Integrated Food Security Phase Classification (IPC), via HDX (`ipc_hdx`)
- **Rows:** 1,014 held · 1,014 a question can reach · 1,014 exportable · 0 older values kept
- **Span:** 2023-11-01 → 2026-07-01 · **series:** 260 · **cadence:** irregular
- **Freshness:** fresh: read on its own rhythm and within its allowance (last information 2026-07-01)
- **Terms:** CC0-1.0 · partners: yes (open) · terms: https://data.humdata.org/dataset/state-of-palestine-acute-food-insecurity-country-data
- **Credit:** Acute food insecurity: IPC (Integrated Food Security Phase Classification), via HDX.
- **Scope:** Each IPC analysis since December 2023: the current period and its projection, the people and the share in phases 1 to 5 and 3 or worse, by the areas that analysis used (single governorates, combined areas, the Strip). The West Bank rows analyse nobody and are not held.
- **Read before use:** An IPC acute food insecurity analysis for the Gaza Strip (National IPC Technical Working Group, via HDX): the people estimated in each phase over the analysis's period, dated by the period's first day (its last day is in the row, with the analysis's month). Each analysis states the current period and a projection for the next. IPC's areas change between analyses, so each area is a series of its own; combined areas (the northern governorates, Deir al-Balah with Khan Younis) are never one governorate. In the May 2024 analysis the file names its combined areas "North Gaza" and "Deir Al-Balah"; their populations show they are the combined areas, and they are held as such. Phase 5 at area level is catastrophe; famine is declared for an area by IPC's Famine Review Committee.
- **Read before use:** An IPC acute food insecurity analysis for the Gaza Strip (National IPC Technical Working Group, via HDX): the people estimated in each phase over the analysis's period, dated by the period's first day (its last day is in the row, with the analysis's month). Each analysis states the current period and a projection for the next. IPC's areas change between analyses, so each area is a series of its own; combined areas (the northern governorates, Deir al-Balah with Khan Younis) are never one governorate. In the May 2024 analysis the file names its combined areas "North Gaza" and "Deir Al-Balah"; their populations show they are the combined areas, and they are held as such. Phase 5 at area level is catastrophe; famine is declared for an area by IPC's Famine Review Committee. This is the analysis's PROJECTION for the period: what IPC expected, not what it measured.
- **Ask:** MCP `databank(category="food", indicator="ipc.population_analysed_gaza_strip")` · REST `/v2/databank/food?indicator=ipc.population_analysed_gaza_strip`
